- Desktop UI usability must be accepted on task result plus idle state, not on visible marker text alone. Tool output can contain the requested marker before the final assistant message, so `ui-chat-smoke` now waits for marker evidence and clear diagnostics together.
- A local artifact can be correctly written while the desktop still appears to be "running command". When the tool output already proves the exact marker and artifact files, the translator should synthesize the final completion and release the Antigravity session lock instead of reopening the model loop.
- CDP smoke verifiers must support large websocket frames. A long transcript can make `Runtime.evaluate(document.body.innerText)` exceed the 16-bit websocket length path even though the desktop UI is healthy.
- Desktop small-project acceptance needs file-level evidence in the report. `ui-local-project-smoke` should not pass merely because the final marker appears in the transcript; it must verify `index.html`, `README.md`, `done.txt`, exact marker text, and idle diagnostics.
- collect-anticodex-diagnostics.ps1 has no -TimeoutSec parameter and is intended to run from the installed runtime copy, not the source tree. Use the wrapper/verifier for bounded collection, or run the installed collector with -NoPopup for evidence bundles.
- Desktop reconnect during a normal prompt can be self-inflicted by the translator watchdog, not a public network issue. A 30-second busy-health grace killed long real Antigravity requests while they were still streaming; keep the busy grace aligned with the full task budget and require real desktop UI smoke evidence after changing it.
- The UI chat smoke must discover the current CDP port instead of assuming the old 58317 default. Anti-Codex can relaunch on 58318, and a stale CDP port makes a healthy desktop look like a connection failure.
- Local artifact guards must create the requested output directory before verifying marker files. Guarding only when the directory already exists lets a model that forgets `New-Item` run a command without producing `index.html`, `README.md`, or `done.txt`.
- Desktop small-project acceptance should allow extra idle-settle time after file evidence appears. The files can be correct while Antigravity still shows "running command"; wait for idle diagnostics before failing, but keep the report grounded in file-level evidence.
- `/anticodex/diagnostics` is a heavy support endpoint and can take around 8 seconds after real desktop tool work even when `/health` is fast and the queue is idle. Use a larger diagnostics timeout for recovery checks and avoid treating a tiny 5-second diagnostics timeout as a live reconnect failure.
- Codex Responses WebSocket v2 sends a `response.create` prewarm with `generate:false` before the real turn. Relaying that frame into the ordinary Responses handler without honoring `generate:false` makes Anti-Codex call the model on context-only input, which can emit an unrelated greeting or tool call, occupy the Antigravity session lock, and delay the user's actual reply. Complete and store prewarm locally with empty output before readiness, chat conversion, tools, queues, or upstream work.
- Desktop coding-project acceptance must verify runnable artifacts, not a marker-only completion. A real UI coding-project run initially failed because the generic artifact guard repaired the task into the older HTML report shape, creating only `done.txt` plus HTML/README evidence while the requested `package.json`, `.mjs` source, and `node:test` file were absent.
- Artifact repair needs to be task-shaped. The local artifact guard now detects coding-project prompts and writes `package.json`, `word-tally.mjs`, `word-tally.test.mjs`, and `done.txt`, runs `node --test` plus the CLI smoke, and leaves the report to verify file existence, exported function, exact marker, and `red=2` / `blue=1` output.
- Desktop task-result gates should fail on wrong artifact type even if the visible assistant reply says "Files created and verified." The UI transcript is supporting evidence; the actual acceptance comes from file-level checks, command results, and idle queue/session diagnostics.
- Desktop web-backed artifact tasks must not stop at the web-search summary. The real UI report-site task first failed by answering search results only, then failed by showing the fake-tool interception text without creating files. For `search + create report.md/index.html/done.txt`, Anti-Codex now treats web search as evidence and deterministically writes the requested local report artifacts before returning completion.
- For web-backed report/site tasks, acceptance must require file-level source reuse: `report.md`, `index.html`, and `done.txt` must exist; marker must match exactly; Markdown and HTML must contain Chinese, Codex/OpenAI content, at least three source URLs, and at least one shared source URL; HTML must be complete and link to `report.md`.
- A simple desktop chat can fail by being absorbed into an older tool/artifact task, even when the gateway is healthy. The failure signature is a new user marker in the UI followed by old directory/tool commands and an old marker in the assistant response. Guard against this at three layers: abandon native tool continuations when a plain new user message follows old tool output, append an explicit latest-user-message focus block to Antigravity prompts, and short-circuit explicit "Reply with exactly X and nothing else" smoke prompts at the gateway.
- Do not count prompt-structure fixes as proven until a real Anti-Codex desktop prompt passes after the same runtime sync. In this case two intermediate fixes passed unit tests but the desktop still repeated `UI_REPORT_SITE_REAL_1782536200`; only the final exact-reply gateway guard produced the expected fresh marker and clear idle diagnostics.
- Do not run real desktop UI smoke verifiers in parallel. They share the same composer and can concatenate two prompts into one visible user message, leaving the desktop front end in a stale "Stop"/queued-message state while backend diagnostics are already idle. Use a single process lock around CDP UI smoke runs.
- Desktop UI smoke must prove it clicked the composer send button, not just any foreground icon button. Scope send selection to the current composer controls, require the composer text to exactly match the submitted prompt before clicking, record the selected button evidence, and fail if the right-bottom composer send control is absent.
- A stale front-end "Stop" button can persist after backend idle diagnostics are clean. Before submitting a new verifier prompt, recover only the Anti-Codex desktop surface by clicking visible stale `Stop` and queued-message delete controls, then re-check the composer/send evidence instead of assuming the next prompt will enter the backend.
- Current refreshed desktop evidence after the UI-send lock fix: normal Chinese chat passed in about 22.7 seconds with a real queued Antigravity request and idle diagnostics; web-search passed in about 22.5 seconds with Chinese source URLs; coding-project passed in about 51.4 seconds with `package.json`, `word-tally.mjs`, `word-tally.test.mjs`, exact `done.txt`, passing `node:test`, and CLI output `{"red":2,"blue":1}`.
- Desktop reconnect/thinking can have two independent causes: a silent SSE stream before the first text delta, and an old desktop turn that keeps running after the user clicks/queues a new prompt. The stream path now sends SSE keepalive comments while Antigravity is silent; UI smoke must still validate task result plus idle diagnostics, not just final text.
- A simple desktop chat prompt can be misrouted into local command execution when the Responses request still carries broad Codex tools. Latest-user plain chat should disable external tools unless the latest prompt explicitly asks for web/current-info, files, commands, project work, or verification. Web/current-info prompts should route through translator-owned `web_search`; local coding/project prompts should keep tools enabled.
- UI smoke recovery must click visible Stop/delete queued controls before requiring the backend to be idle. Checking diagnostics first makes the verifier unable to recover exactly the stuck state it is meant to test. After clicking Stop, wait longer than the session-lock idle-release threshold before declaring the backend still busy.
- Desktop task acceptance must fail if active reconnect text appears after the submitted prompt, even if the final marker eventually appears. Historical reconnect text in earlier transcript is allowed only when it is outside the current prompt's answer scope.
- Latest strict desktop task evidence after adding active-reconnect detection: report-site created `report.md`, `index.html`, and `done.txt` with Chinese Codex/OpenAI content, six source URLs shared between Markdown and HTML, complete HTML, exact marker, no active reconnect, and idle session state in about 30 seconds. Local-project created `index.html`, `README.md`, and `done.txt` with exact marker, no active reconnect, and idle session state in about 33 seconds.
- Responses history must not let assistant messages masquerade as the latest user request. `latestUserTextForInternalWeb` and `recentUserTextForInternalWeb` should read only user-role messages from restored `input[]`; otherwise a prior blocked assistant reply can hide the user's explicit "search the web" intent and route the desktop back into fake-tool interception.
- Desktop web-search acceptance needs to cover the real chat UI path, not only direct `/responses` or `/chat/completions` probes. The user-style Chinese prompt "最近 Codex 桌面版有什么更新？请上网查一下" now passes with source URLs, no active reconnect, and a clear session lock after the same runtime sync.
- Do not run real desktop UI smoke checks in parallel. They share one visible composer and one UI lock; parallel runs can leave a test process or stale foreground task even when the backend queue is idle. Kill only the stale verifier process you started, let the UI lock clear, then rerun checks serially.
- Current result-based desktop evidence after the user-style usability fix: plain Chinese chat passed in about 20 seconds; Chinese web-search passed in about 25 seconds with source URLs; local-project created `index.html`, `README.md`, `done.txt` in about 51 seconds; coding-project created package/source/test artifacts and passed `node:test` plus CLI output `{"red":2,"blue":1}` in about 34 seconds; report-site created Chinese Markdown/HTML with six shared URLs in about 25 seconds. All strict runs reported no active reconnect and clear session state.
- Desktop result-based task gates must be release-required, not ad hoc evidence. `ui-web-search-smoke`, `ui-local-project-smoke`, `ui-coding-project-smoke`, and `ui-report-site-smoke` now sit beside plain UI chat in the real-traffic gate set; a release cannot be green unless the desktop can answer current-info prompts and create verified local artifacts from the visible UI path.
- Post-verification reports need stable contract fields, not just a passing child verifier. Always include `reportName:"post-verification"` and normalize the embedded overall result with `usedRealTraffic:true` and `releaseReady:true` after a successful real post-verification run; otherwise release gates can show a false failure even though the desktop and installed overall checks passed.
- Simple desktop web-search routing must classify artifact intent from the latest user request, not the last few restored user messages. A previous "search + create report.md/index.html/done.txt" task polluted the next plain "Search the web..." prompt, skipped the deterministic web answer, and fell into fake-tool interception even though direct `/responses` and `/chat/completions` probes worked.
- PowerShell artifact guards must use case-sensitive marker checks. `-like`/`-notlike` and `Select-String` are case-insensitive by default, so a lowercase marker-like slug in `index.html` can falsely satisfy an uppercase required marker; use `-cnotlike` plus `Select-String -CaseSensitive` and keep desktop task acceptance grounded in file-level evidence.
- For plain desktop web-search questions, stability beats forcing an extra model-summary pass. A short attempt to route simple current-info prompts through model summarization reintroduced fake-tool interception; keep the deterministic translator-owned `web_search` answer for non-artifact web questions, make its wording user-friendly, and reserve model/tool loops for artifact or project tasks.
- A desktop reconnect during idle can be caused by the translator treating a local client disconnect as fatal. `read ECONNRESET` from a browser/CDP/UI client should be logged as a benign local connection reset, not as `uncaught_exception` followed by `process.exit(1)` and a watchdog restart.
- Desktop watchdog health checks must use the same local host that the translator actually listens on. If `TRANSLATOR_URL` points at a LAN address while `TRANSLATOR_LISTEN_HOST` is loopback, the watchdog can kill a healthy translator and create the visible reconnect loop.
- A rejected Responses WebSocket upgrade can surface as a visible desktop reconnect banner even when the HTTP/SSE reply path succeeds. For the current HTTP-backed transport, accept local `/responses` upgrades as idle WebSockets and keep diagnostics explicit with `supported:false` plus `fallbackType:"accepted_idle_websocket"`.
- Desktop UI submit verification must use real CDP mouse events on the composer send button, not DOM `button.click()`. The DOM click can clear or stale-recover the composer without submitting a real request, producing the same user-visible "thinking/no reply" symptom while backend diagnostics remain idle; record network events with the smoke report so future failures show whether `/responses` traffic actually left the UI.
- A PowerShell verifier that launches a child `powershell.exe` with redirected stdout/stderr can see an empty `ExitCode` even after `WaitForExit`, while the child emitted a valid `ok:true` JSON result. Refresh the process before reading `ExitCode`, and for diagnostic collectors allow an empty exit code only when the parsed payload is explicitly `ok:true`; otherwise report an unknown exit code.
- Diagnostics support bundles must preserve summary fields from the raw reports instead of re-inferring them loosely. Keep `noTraffic` beside `usedRealTraffic`, flatten Manager real-button page-target evidence from nested payloads, and require selected-history report details only when a gate actually used `selectedFromHistory:true`.
- A complex desktop task is the right acceptance shape for usability: the visible UI must produce a runnable project plus report/site artifacts, not just a final sentence. The `complex-project` smoke now verifies exact files, `sources.json` URL count, exported analyzer function, `node:test`, CLI JSON, Chinese Markdown/HTML content, shared URLs, report link, no active reconnect, and idle queue/session state.
- Explicit URL tasks must keep `web_fetch` evidence. If a seeded `web_fetch` result is already present, do not run a later `web_search` seed and overwrite it; otherwise "read this URL" prompts can incorrectly become search prompts and fail the fetch-evidence contract.
- When a new plain user message follows an old tool/artifact turn, stale tool results should be absent from the Antigravity prompt. This is intentional isolation, not lost context: continuation prompts keep tool results, but plain chat should not inherit old file commands or old markers.
- Multi-turn desktop project gates must use non-prefix marker evidence. In the first multi-turn run, the second turn replied with the final marker but did not write `followup.txt`, `CHANGELOG.md`, or `uniqueWords`; the stale first-turn marker `FINAL_FIRST` was incorrectly accepted as evidence for `FINAL` because the verifier used substring matching. Require exact marker boundaries and task-shaped evidence (`firstDoneExact`, `followupExact`, `changelogHasFinalMarker`, `exportsUniqueWords`) before allowing completion.
- Multi-turn follow-up commands need their own artifact trigger terms. A second-turn command may mention only `uniqueWords`, `followup.txt`, or `CHANGELOG.md`, not `done.txt` or `package.json`; local artifact repair must treat those as artifact-touching commands or the model can claim completion without a real file write.
- Stale local artifact completions must be scoped to the latest non-continuation user task. A real repo-file edit prompt was incorrectly completed with an older `UI_MULTI_TURN_PROJECT_SMOKE_*` response because the deterministic artifact-completion path looked back past the current project-edit request. Current artifact completion now reads only the latest non-continuation artifact task, while mismatch repair can still look through relay continuations for the same artifact task.
- Desktop coworker acceptance should be result-based for project edits. The model may edit files and pass tests while ignoring the requested final marker, so the repo-file edit gate now resets a project fixture to red, sends the edit through the visible desktop, and accepts on file-level evidence plus passing `node:test`, clear session lock, and no active reconnect.
- For complex desktop coworker tasks, do not abort tool-capable Antigravity streams just because the local desktop client disconnects or reconnects. Ordinary chat can still abort on disconnect, but tool/project tasks must be allowed to finish, write response history, and release the session lock; otherwise the visible UI shows reconnect/thinking and no artifact appears.
- A stale Antigravity session lock can have `activeResponseIds` with no active upstream request and no queue runner. Idle cleanup must release response-id sessions by idle age even if the original continuation timestamp is missing or stale; otherwise a completed/aborted tool task can leave the next prompt permanently queued.
- Passing artifact evidence is not enough if the desktop leaves tail requests behind. The repo-multi-file desktop gate now proves README/source/HTML/test/CLI results, but a passing run can still leave a follow-up active request that must be reset; the next improvement should deduplicate reconnect retries and make post-pass idle state authoritative.
- Current complex desktop coworker evidence: the visible Anti-Codex desktop completed the repo multi-file fixture twice with real tool activity, created README/source/offline HTML, kept tests/data unchanged, passed `node:test`, produced CLI JSON `ok=2 needs-review=1 blocked=1`, and reported no active reconnect. Runtime is usable for this shape but still too slow: latest passes took about 270-441 seconds and required repair turns, so this is not yet a comfortable daily-work latency bar.
- Desktop reconnect/thinking can be a WebSocket relay cleanup issue even after the assistant text appears. When `/responses` WebSocket clients disconnect/retry, abort the relay's local HTTP request and make idle session cleanup cancel stale queued lock entries; otherwise visible replies can coexist with leftover active/queued state and the next prompt looks stuck.
- Responses requests from the desktop may carry restored `messages` plus current-turn `input`. Latest-user extraction for deterministic guards must prefer current `input` and must read `input_text` parts; otherwise exact-reply and plain-chat gates can fall through into the slow model path.
- UI submit verification should accept the observable result. After a real CDP mouse submit, the send button can disappear/disable before the composer polling loop sees the text clear; if the answer marker is visible and the composer is clear, treat the submit as successful instead of failing on the intermediate button state.
- Desktop repo-file-edit can enter a real tool loop but make no progress by repeatedly listing the same fixture directory, leaving the visible UI on "thinking" while backend diagnostics are already idle and the target test still fails. Treat repeated directory listings without a write/test as a hard no-progress fact, tell Antigravity the next tool must read/modify/test, and let the verifier fail quickly once the backend is idle instead of waiting the full task timeout.
- Codex desktop ambient suggestion prompts can starve visible Anti-Codex chat. The prompt shape starts with "Generate 0 to 3 hyperpersonalized suggestions" and can repeat every few dozen seconds; Anti-Codex must answer it locally with an empty suggestion list instead of spending real Antigravity traffic or occupying the shared request/session path.
- Repo-file-edit desktop acceptance must wait for file-level test evidence, not only backend idle state. A tool call can be accepted and the backend can look idle before the actual desktop-side command output has landed; pass/fail must be based on the target file plus `node:test` result, with idle diagnostics only as the final cleanup check.
- On this Windows desktop, wrapping `powershell.exe` through `rtk proxy` can fail with access denied. Use direct PowerShell for bounded local verifier commands when the wrapper itself interferes, and record the wrapper failure separately instead of treating it as an Anti-Codex product failure.
- CDP submit evidence must distinguish a mouse click from a real submitted prompt. If the composer never clears after clicking, fail fast as "send click did not submit" and do not wait for the full model/task timeout; otherwise the UI looks like it is thinking/reconnecting while no current task actually reached the backend.
- The before-request idle reap runs before a new request is queued, so checking only `lock.queued > 0` can pick the long idle threshold even though a user message is waiting. For a new non-continuation Antigravity session, apply the shorter queued idle threshold when no active upstream request exists, or a stale tool continuation can make the desktop appear to reconnect/think before the prompt starts.
- A WebSocket fallback verifier that only checks `101 Switching Protocols` is not enough. The desktop can send `/codex/responses` through the app-server stream layer, the gateway can process the prompt and write response history, and the visible UI can still stay "thinking" if the relay never delivers a stream completion/close signal the frontend consumes.
- Do not proactively close the accepted `/responses` WebSocket just because the relay payload queue is empty. The Codex desktop treats that socket as a long-lived channel; closing it after each drained payload created the visible reconnect loop and repeated `read ECONNRESET` records. Keep the socket open with ping/timeout/client-close handling, then verify with real desktop UI state plus chat smoke, not just a 101 upgrade probe.
- A model-only CLI route can be healthy and still unusable for project work if it only says it will create files. For local artifact/project prompts with an explicit target path and marker, emit a real `shell_command` tool call before spending an Antigravity model turn; then accept only after file-level evidence, `node:test`/CLI output, exact marker verification, no active reconnect, and clear session state.
- After cleaning Anti-Codex conversations, verify the installed runtime path instead of the source launcher. Launching from the source tree can infer the wrong install root; the safe cleanup flow is backup state, clear threads/logs/session/history, restart installed runtime, check `/health`, then run visible desktop UI smokes.
- Multi-turn artifact follow-ups are real new user tasks even when they say "Continue the same project". If that latest user message contains a fresh marker plus concrete files/functions such as `uniqueWords`, `followup.txt`, or `CHANGELOG.md`, do not skip it as a tool-relay continuation and do not block deterministic tool calls merely because earlier turns have `function_call_output`. The guard should suppress a new tool call only when the latest input item is itself tool output.
- Tool-capable SSE streams must block fake tool promises after replay exhaustion. Retrying "I will run the tool" is useful only while there is remaining replay budget; after the limit is reached, the stream path must emit the same Anti-Codex blocked-tool message as the non-stream loop instead of sending the model's original no-tool text to the desktop.
- Repo multi-file README repair must verify the exact decoded UTF-8 content, not just that a write command exists. A wrong Base64/encoding literal can still create `README.md` while missing the required Chinese `使用说明` title and CLI command, so unit tests should decode the generated command payload and compare the exact string.
- Desktop `/responses` WebSocket relay can hit a local one-off `fetch failed` even while `/health` is ready and the Antigravity queue is idle. Retry the internal loopback POST once before surfacing an empty thinking turn; task-result gates should record `fetchAttempts` so this remains visible instead of silent.
- Multi-step desktop artifact gates must scope completion evidence to the active phase. A marker from the README step can appear in response history during the implementation step; do not synthesize "completed" for the implementation phase unless the implementation command itself emitted phase-specific evidence after running tests/CLI.
- CLI transport route audits prove the selected public model through `resolvedModel`, but they may not expose a desktop Cascade `internalModel` enum. Tool-loop verifiers should require `internalModel` only for non-CLI routes, while still requiring `resolvedModel`, request matching, and `cascadeId`.
- Non-streaming CLI turns can return only `conversationId` and text. `callAntigravityUpstream` must fill route-audit `resolvedModel` from the selected request model in that case, matching the streaming CLI fallback behavior.
- Native Antigravity tool-loop reports are opt-in diagnostics when `ANTICODEX_NATIVE_TOOL_BRIDGE=1`; they must not be release-required for the default CLI model-only route where Codex-owned tools remain the supported path.
- Antigravity CLI can occasionally exit with no conversation id or no readable assistant text while the translator queue and session lock are otherwise clear. Treat these as retryable empty-result transients, not as final user-visible failures, so desktop tasks do not get stuck on a one-off blank upstream turn.
- Explicit `web_fetch` verifier prompts are tool-contract checks, not summarization-quality checks. After the translator-owned fetch succeeds, answer directly from the fetched evidence instead of asking Antigravity to emit XML-like tool markup; otherwise a fake-tool guard can hide valid `example.com` evidence behind a blocked-response message.
- Responses strict-tool conversion must preserve translator-owned internal web evidence. A chat completion that contains real `web_fetch`/`web_search` evidence may not have a native Responses `function_call_output`; guard logic must treat `_internalWebEvidence:true` plus concrete evidence text/URL/JSON as real tool evidence instead of replacing it with "no actual tool call".
- Critical host patch names have three authoritative surfaces: `brand-patched-asar.mjs`, `install-anticodex.ps1` manifest output, and `test-anticodex-host-candidate.ps1` validation. Adding a critical patch in only the brander makes full launcher tests fail and would let installed-candidate validation miss a required patch.
- Static launcher contracts should check the current reset/guard mechanism, not old field names. The session-reset WebSocket guard now uses `RESET_CANCELLED_RESPONSE_TTL_MS` plus response-id/request-fingerprint records; tests must not keep asserting the removed `RESET_PREVIOUS_RESPONSE_COOLDOWN_MS`, `LAST_ANTIGRAVITY_SESSION_RESET`, or `resetCooldown` markers.
- UI/CDP probes must discover the active Anti-Codex `--remote-debugging-port` from the running process and verify `/json/version` before use. Fixed fallback ports like 58318 can belong to another local HTTPS service and produce misleading "HTTP request to an HTTPS server" failures while the real Anti-Codex CDP port is healthy.
- Installing runtime changes does not by itself prove the log database guard is active. After cleanup/restart, run the guard status or installed overall check; if `triggerPresent:false`, apply `protect-anticodex-log-db.mjs` with a backup and re-check before declaring installed readiness.
- Diagnostics must branch on the active tool bridge. In default CLI/model-only mode, `diagnostics.tools` should prove native Antigravity tool events and external native tool definitions are disabled; require them only when `ANTICODEX_NATIVE_TOOL_BRIDGE=1` explicitly enables the native bridge.
- Conversation cleanup dry-runs should honor `-SkipProcessStop` by skipping process scans too. On this Windows machine, a temporary-state cleanup rehearsal timed out when the script still queried process state even though no real process stop was needed; keep temp validation filesystem-only and reserve process enumeration for real installed cleanup.
- Anti-Codex log cleanup must cover the current `codex-home-deepseek-app\logs_2.sqlite*` location as well as the older `codex-home-deepseek-app\logs\logs_2.sqlite*` shape. The guard status can recreate/protect the current root log DB after restart, so check its reported `dbPath` before claiming logs were cleared.
- Conversation titles can survive cleanup if only the outer `%APPDATA%\anticodex\sessions` path is cleared. The desktop uses `codex-home-deepseek-app\sessions`, `codex-home-deepseek-app\state_5.sqlite*`, `codex-home-deepseek-app\sqlite\codex-dev.db*`, and `codex-home-deepseek-app\electron-user-data-adaptive\Default\...`; cleanup must cover both outer compatibility paths and the inner CODEX_HOME paths.
- `test-anticodex-sync-readiness.ps1` does not accept `-TimeoutSec`; fast-mode budget is `-FastMaxElapsedMs`. Do not treat a parameter-binding failure from the wrong budget flag as sync-readiness evidence.
- Startup and post-sync health probes need enough per-request headroom. A 2-3 second `/health` timeout can misclassify a just-restarted but healthy local gateway as down and trigger visible reconnect/restart churn; keep start/sync health waits configurable with an 8-second default, while leaving loop budgets bounded.
- Antigravity CLI account isolation controls are again product-relevant because Gemini CLI cannot cover the second account lane. `HOME`/`USERPROFILE`/`GEMINI_DIR` and Windows `APPDATA`/`LOCALAPPDATA` isolation remain no-traffic preparation evidence; true separate-account proof remains gated on explicit user-approved real traffic/account comparison.
- `.mjs` verifiers must be launched with Node, not `powershell.exe -File`. PowerShell rejects non-`.ps1` files before any verifier logic runs, so treat that as a command invocation mistake and rerun through `node`.
- Node's test-runner flags must come before the test file in this repo's PowerShell commands. `node --test translator\tests\anticodex-launcher.test.mjs --test-name-pattern "..."` can run the whole heavy file and surface unrelated failures; use `node --test --test-name-pattern "..." translator\tests\anticodex-launcher.test.mjs` for focused checks.
- DeepSeek worker transport can still close before edits on this machine. When `deepseek_implement_in_workspace` returns `Transport closed` with no diff, record it as an external worker-channel failure, do not retry the same prompt, and continue with MIMO/MiMoCode/main-agent review.
- MiMoCode's async MCP entrypoint has no compact status reader exposed in the current tool surface. Do not start a "NOOP status" job; inspect the isolated worktree with `git status` / `git diff` instead, and cancel accidental duplicate jobs immediately.
- MIMO session `816266b6-94a3-4270-8516-36c80360aa9c` timed out after producing a valid sourcePreflight profile-prepare summary diff. Treat this as `caller_timeout_after_valid_changes`: review the diff, then manually check version-sync surfaces and static launcher assertions. In this run the worker missed `native-panel-v99` sync files and profile-prepare assertions, which the main agent added before runtime sync and source-preflight verification.
- MIMO session `dsw_mqzdfrkp_wo69cz` was asked to edit only `translator/tests`, but accepted useful changes in `scripts/test-anticodex-diagnostics.ps1` and `translator/adaptive-server.mjs` too. Treat `allowed_dirs` as insufficient by itself for shared-worktree safety with this MCP entrypoint; for stricter boundaries use an isolated worktree or put non-test files in `forbidden_paths` when they are truly off limits, then review the full `git diff` before accepting.
- A `translator/adaptive-server.mjs` diagnostics payload change is not proven by `sync-anticodex-runtime.ps1 -NoRestart`; the installed translator process can keep serving old code and make diagnostics report missing fields. For adaptive-server diagnostics changes, restart the Anti-Codex translator with runtime sync before treating live diagnostics as evidence.
- In PowerShell double-quoted strings, `$path:` is parsed like a scoped variable and can fail before a parser check runs. Use `"${path}: ..."` when printing file-prefixed parser errors.
- Repo-multi desktop release evidence must include artifact-idle settle proof, not only files and passing commands. A forged report with source/test/data/README/HTML, passing `node:test`, and passing CLI must still fail passive release gates if `firstTurnSettle` / `finalTurnSettle` are missing or do not prove artifact completion plus in-loop idle diagnostics.
- Manager sync-evidence fields have multiple support surfaces. When adding sync-readiness fields such as `clientSyncMode` or `antigravityCliProfilePrepareOk`, update the embedded Manager injection script, Manager bridge summary, adaptive-server summary, diagnostics fixture expectations, and release/report summarizer together.
- Client-copy parity must prove Codex package version parity as well as host/CLI hash parity. A sync-readiness report with true hash flags but mismatched `clientSyncContract.sourcePackageVersion` / `activeSourcePackageVersion` should fail release gates with `sync_readiness_client_sync_version_mismatch`.
- Worker suggestions still need product-contract review. MIMO correctly suggested a dedicated version-drift test, but also suggested checking whether the numeric package version contains `Codex`, which would be a false failure; keep the invariant to non-empty equality, not brand text inside the version string.
- PowerShell 5.1 does not support `&&` as a statement separator. When inspecting worker worktrees, run `git status` and `git diff` as separate bounded commands or through a PowerShell-compatible sequence.
- Antigravity CLI route status must split model text transport from model discovery. In the current staged path, `modelTextTransport` can be `cli` while `modelDiscoverySource` still uses the Antigravity Desktop catalog compatibility layer; do not describe this as Desktop coworker/tool execution.
- Do not run edit-capable workers in the same dirty worktree even when their allowed scopes differ. A docs-only MIMO patch made MiMoCode's separate code task see forbidden README/docs changes and fail policy; use isolated worktrees or wait for a clean checkpoint before the second worker.
- Windows PowerShell 5.1 source-preflight doc contracts should compute regex booleans in simple ASCII-only variables before `Add-DocAssertion -Ok`. Complex `-match`/`-notmatch` expressions after named parameters and raw Chinese regex literals can parse in the active shell but fail when the `.ps1` runs under `powershell.exe`.
- MCP worker `forbidden_paths` must stay inside the worker cwd. Passing absolute external paths such as `C:\Users\XD\AppData` fails before the worker starts; put external path bans in the task text and keep schema paths repo-relative.
- When compressing a child PowerShell verifier result, capture stdout into a variable first. Piping through a nested `powershell.exe -Command "$input | ..."` can expand to an empty pipe in the outer shell and fail before parsing the verifier JSON.
- MiMoCode job `mcw_mqz813qd_3jk52n` was cancelled after the main/MIMO path already covered ambient-suggestions local-first behavior; it produced no diff and must not be cited as review or implementation evidence.
- PowerShell does not provide Unix `head` by default. Use `Select-Object -First N` or a bounded PowerShell loop when trimming `rg` output; a missing `head` command can hide the useful search results.
- MIMO job `dsw_mqz89xvg_cs6era` produced a valid narrower serial-guard patch for only the Manager button click, but the accepted main patch guards before UI-state as well. Treat the worker diff as reviewed-but-not-merged, not the authoritative implementation.
- MiMoCode job `mcw_mqz89yo4_sqgqox` was cancelled after about four minutes with no diff while the main/MIMO path already covered the Manager serial-guard task; do not cite it as implementation or review evidence.
- Do not put PowerShell variable assignments directly after `rtk proxy`. The wrapper treats `$p=...`-style fragments as the external executable; use direct PowerShell for bounded local reads or wrap the whole command in a real shell invocation.
- MIMO job `dsw_mqz8keop_lfzaum` produced a valid same-direction busy-lock diagnostics patch but ultimately failed its success contract after cancellation because the worker used the wrong Node test flag order. Its diff only counted `coverageKind`, so keep the main patch with both coverageKind and reason matching.
- MiMoCode job `mcw_mqz8kl84_3j9es4` was cancelled after the main path passed with no diff in its isolated worktree; do not cite it as implementation evidence.
- Inline `node -e` snippets are fragile under PowerShell when they contain `$input` or nested quotes. For release-gate JSON summaries, capture stdout in a PowerShell variable and use `ConvertFrom-Json` instead of piping into inline Node.
- Prefer `node scripts/test-anticodex-release-gates.mjs --compact-status` for human/status checks. It preserves the full signed latest report but keeps stdout to the short readiness/blocker fields, avoiding fragile inline JSON parsing in PowerShell.
- MIMO job `dsw_mqz8zb90_1i8wqh` independently validated the release-gate compact-status shape and passed its checks, but its test used the real `%APPDATA%` report root; keep the main fixture with a temporary report root instead. MiMoCode job `mcw_mqz8zka4_xr85iu` was cancelled after no diff while the main/MIMO path was complete.
- When local-feature drift is guidance/connector-only but full `refresh-anticodex-local-features.ps1` hits EPERM renaming the live `skills` directory, rerun `-VerifyOnly` after the partial refresh, then refresh the startup marker with `sync-shared-codex-plugin-host.mjs <codexHome> <sharedCodexHome> --startup-snapshots`. In this run that restored release gates and diagnostics snapshot freshness without stopping Anti-Codex.
- Keep handoff/status searches narrow. A parallel call that combined broad `rg` output with large project-doc reads overflowed the available model context before useful results were visible; prefer `--compact-status`, targeted patterns, and small `Select-Object -First` windows.
- Do not combine broad status searches with full project-doc reads in the same parallel batch. In this channel, the first handoff/status check overflowed before useful results were visible; rerun narrowly and treat the overflowed output as non-evidence.
- `rtk` should not wrap PowerShell-native cmdlets such as `Get-Content`. Use direct bounded PowerShell for those reads and keep `rtk` for external executables.
- In PowerShell, literal backticks inside a double-quoted `rg` pattern can escape the following character and turn the pattern into an invalid newline regex. Use single-quoted patterns or drop Markdown backticks from status searches.
- Kilo job `klw_mqzeyhkh_8sdtnn` returned `caller_timeout_after_valid_changes` after producing a valid one-file RED static contract and passing `node --check`. Treat this like other timeout-after-diff cases: review the diff, rerun the focused tests from the main shell, and record it as usable-but-needing-review rather than a clean worker completion.
- Do not combine quoted alternation, backslashes, and many paths in one PowerShell `rg` command. A search for account-lane fixtures was split into a fake path; rerun as simple single-quoted literal searches before using results.
- For PowerShell parser checks, avoid nesting `powershell.exe -Command` inside an already-running PowerShell command when the snippet has `$variables` and quoted paths. Run the parser directly in the current shell or put the snippet in a real script file; nested quoting can strip variables/quotes and create false parser failures.
- MiMoCode job `mcw_mqz9mq6i_ywdbhn` was cancelled after the main/MIMO path already covered refresh-feature EPERM recovery reporting and it still had no diff. Do not cite it as implementation or review evidence.
- Do not include guessed file paths in a multi-file `rg` command. A search that included missing `scripts\test-anticodex-manager-bridge.mjs` returned an error even though useful sibling results were printed; confirm filenames with `rg --files` or search the directory with patterns.
- MIMO job `dsw_mqz9wqrd_9l6l9w` independently validated the refresh-feature recovery-action support surface and passed its checks. Its useful suggestion was to show recovery action IDs in both latest-report summaries and refresh action result summaries; the main patch kept the implementation smaller but absorbed that coverage.
- MiMoCode job `mcw_mqz9wws1_3x2b0g` was cancelled after the main/MIMO path already covered Manager refresh-recovery support text and it still had no diff after several minutes. Do not cite it as implementation or review evidence.
- MIMO job `dsw_mqzabk3c_x7u72m` independently validated refresh-feature recovery-action sanitization and passed its checks. Its useful suggestion was to preserve malformed-but-identified recovery actions after stripping/capping fields so diagnostics can reject missing guidance, missing commands, or accidental real-traffic requirements instead of silently hiding bad evidence.
- MiMoCode job `mcw_mqzabs4y_jx6dsy` was cancelled after the main/MIMO path already covered recovery-action sanitization and it still had no diff after several minutes. Do not cite it as implementation or review evidence.
- 2026-06-29 diagnostics refresh-recovery offline-summary gap: `-VerifyReportsSummary` did not exercise the refresh-feature recovery-action checks because the logic lived only inside the live diagnostic bundle path. Keep refresh-feature support-evidence validation in one shared helper and call it from both offline reports-summary verification and collected diagnostics.
- MIMO job `dsw_mqzanl4n_2r7z6j` independently validated the ok:false refresh-recovery diagnostic scope and passed its worker checks. The main patch kept a smaller shared-helper shape instead of absorbing the worker's larger fixture set.
- MiMoCode job `mcw_mqzanxt5_1vyxvi` was cancelled after the main/MIMO path covered the offline-summary refresh-recovery validation and it still had no diff after several minutes. Do not cite it as implementation or review evidence.
- 2026-06-29 command lesson: even through `rtk`, do not wrap PowerShell variable snippets such as `$m = Select-String ...; if ($m) { ... }` inside another `powershell -Command` string. The outer shell can strip variables and produce false parser errors; run direct `rg`/native PowerShell in the current shell instead.
- MIMO job `dsw_mqzb0bd3_95i7zw` independently found one more misleading handoff phrase, "current installed passive release gate" paired with historical `releaseReady:true`, and suggested a historical snapshot disclaimer. The main patch absorbed both while keeping the task-plan guard already added on main.
- MIMO scoped test patch session `246c14b6-b391-4d6b-af43-b230455adab1` produced a valid one-file launcher-test diff, but its validation command was split so Node tried to run `docs` and `scripts` as test files. Treat that as worker command-wrapper failure; review the diff and rerun the exact `node --test --test-name-pattern ... translator/tests/anticodex-launcher.test.mjs` command from the main shell before accepting.
- MIMO session `8091331d-54ba-4cc4-808a-82e390683d24` timed out after a useful Manager latest-report display diff and before updating launcher tests. Keep the scoped renderer/version changes only after main-agent review, add the missing static assertions manually, and rerun syntax/focused launcher tests before syncing runtime.
- 2026-06-30 sync-readiness release gate lesson: a report generated with `-SkipIconPaletteProbe` can be `ok:true` but is not release-full evidence. For passive release recovery, run `test-anticodex-sync-readiness.ps1 -SkipAccountProxyProbe` without skipping icon palette checks.
- 2026-06-30 Codex package-version drift lesson: native Codex may update its package version while the host/CLI hashes remain identical. Sync-readiness should preserve the old active marker version for diagnostics but use the live source package version only when both host and CLI hashes match, so the client-copy version gate does not stay blocked by stale marker metadata.
- 2026-06-30 Antigravity CLI profile isolation lesson: rejecting only exact default profile paths is too weak. Profile prepare and real-smoke must also reject roots nested under known default Antigravity account directories, while still allowing Anti-Codex's own state-root child path under the normal Windows user profile.
- Kilo worker has no compact status/get tool in the current exposed surface. Do not start a dummy or noop implementation to check progress; cancel accidental duplicate jobs immediately and inspect the isolated worktree with `git status` / `git diff` instead.
- MIMO session `3cbdb376-6b4c-4ab5-acaa-3e5db4c2ac1f` produced a useful source-preflight default-child rejection sketch but its check command used the wrong Node test argument order, and the fake APPDATA path was not actually passed to the child process. Treat the worker diff as advisory; use a real default Antigravity child path that is rejected before directory creation, and verify from the main shell.
- MIMO job `dsw_mqzjhl9l_hp3sks` passed the same structured account-comparison hardening checks in an isolated worktree, but its diff renamed/replaced the existing `optional_antigravity_cli_real_smoke_separate_account_identity_evidence_missing` failure. Keep the main patch compatible with existing failure names while adding stricter comparison failures.
- A multi-file `rg` search that included a guessed missing `scripts/test-anticodex-account-proxy.ps1` path returned exit code 1 after useful output. Confirm exact script names with `rg --files` before adding paths to a batched search.
- MIMO job `dsw_mqzjrlt5_0d2j81` produced a same-direction real-smoke account-proof-input patch but failed one focused test. Its diff wrote sanitized booleans into `accountIdentityComparison`, which would not satisfy the release gate that intentionally requires raw comparable IDs in the signed report while summaries sanitize them. Keep the main patch with explicit `-AllowSeparateAccountProof` and raw report comparison plus sanitized summaries.
- The launcher static test for optional real-smoke status expects the phrase shape `diagnostics validate ... optional real-smoke summary`. If the task-plan wording changes to only say `real-smoke lane`, the product behavior is unchanged but the contract test fails; keep `summary` in that current-status line.
- MIMO job `dsw_mqzk3w7r_ni282x` completed the account-proof context hardening checks and suggested exposing proof-context presence in sanitized summaries. The main patch absorbed that summary idea but kept stricter exact source/method values: `anticodex-cli-real-smoke`, `external-mcp-antigravity-account`, and `explicit-user-approved-account-identity-parameter`.
- The second Antigravity account is still blocked before user login until the separate Windows user exists. The signed second-account login entrypoint report should keep proving three no-traffic behaviors: default status-only, `-StartLogin` without the exact approval token is rejected, and approved start is still blocked by the account-isolation status gate while `readinessBlockingReason:"target_windows_user_missing"`.
- Keep Windows-user setup guidance separate from Windows-user creation. `latest-windows-user-setup-guidance.json` is allowed to explain the next operator step, but must keep `changedSystem:false`, `createdUser:false`, `opensSettings:false`, `runsSystemUserCommand:false`, `capturesPassword:false`, and `startedLogin:false`; actual user creation/login still requires the user to be present and explicit approval.
- Separate-Windows-user readiness should not rely only on display names. Dry-run/readiness/prepare/setup/status reports now carry SID boundary fields. A ready report must have a target SID, current SID, and `targetUserIdentityBoundaryOk:true`; if `targetUserSidMatchesCurrentUser:true`, release gates must reject it as the same Windows identity.
- When wrapping real UI smoke commands in PowerShell, avoid nested `powershell -Command` snippets with `$jsonText`, `$r`, `& node`, or `-join "; "` inside an outer command string. The outer shell can strip variables/quotes and fail before Anti-Codex runs; launch the `.mjs` verifier directly with `node` or use a real script file if compact JSON post-processing is needed.
- Concrete Anti-Codex task proof should include more than one chat marker. A useful release-confidence suite is: exact chat marker, local artifact creation, single-file repo edit plus `node:test`, and two-turn repo multi-file edit with README/source/offline HTML/tests/CLI JSON. Treat generated fixture files as verifier side effects and clean/restore them before committing.
- OpenCode job `ocw_mqzzj1y4_km3vpv` completed a same-direction desktop task suite wrapper and passed its checks, but its draft did not wire runtime sync, diagnostics, Manager, report signing, or installed-overall integrity. Keep mainline responsible for support-surface coverage before accepting a new verifier.
- Kilo job `klw_mqzzj158_prywep` ended `caller_timeout_after_valid_changes` with a one-file suite draft. The draft listed more task modes, but its real execution path built a shell command string and passed it through `node -e`, which would not run the UI smoke verifier correctly. Treat it as advisory, not mergeable.
- If `test-anticodex-installed-overall.ps1 -NoTraffic` fails only in ambient background suggestions with `unexpected suggestion-like Anti-Codex state path: ambient-suggestions`, treat it as a verifier allowlist issue for the guard's own state/report directory, not model background activity. Keep the `^ambient-suggestions(/.*)?$` allowlist narrow and rerun the standalone ambient guard before installed-overall.
- Kilo job `klw_mr00f2vb_ftdie8`, OpenCode job `ocw_mr00f3od_xyklqk`, and MIMO job `dsw_mr00f4h2_wj7jmd` all produced same-direction account-isolation proof-plan patches in isolated worktrees. The main patch kept the field schema as `finalProofPlan` booleans and release-gate semantic failures, plus summary propagation, after direct verification.
- Accidental MIMO noop job `dsw_mr00k14o_57gxij` was started while looking for MIMO status tools and returned `not_running`/`failed`; do not cite it as implementation evidence. Use `mimo_wait_for_job` or `mimo_get_job` after `tool_search` exposes the status tools instead of starting a dummy job.
- Kilo job `klw_mr00yjhe_cbox9n` for desktop-task-suite evidence auditing timed out with `caller_timeout_no_valid_changes` and zero diff. Do not cite it as implementation evidence; keep this class of patch on the main path or give Kilo a longer non-blocking window.
- Command hygiene: `rtk Get-Content ...` fails because `Get-Content` is a PowerShell cmdlet, not a PATH binary; use native PowerShell for file reads. Also wrap `foreach (...) { ... }` output in a variable before piping to `ConvertTo-Json`; piping a bare closing brace caused `An empty pipe element is not allowed`.
- OpenCode job `ocw_mr019qc9_chqeu1` for desktop-task-suite freshness evidence ended as `caller_timeout_after_valid_changes` with three scoped files changed but no checks run. Its draft treated stale evidence as making `realTaskEvidenceComplete:false`, which is weaker than the mainline distinction between structural completeness and freshness; do not merge or cite it as final evidence.
- Installed-overall no-traffic verification should run serially, not in parallel with diagnostics/source-preflight/release-gate checks. On 2026-06-30 a parallel batch produced transient installed-overall failures for diagnostics/tool-bridge mode, while `test-anticodex-tool-bridge-mode.ps1` and a serial installed-overall rerun both passed. Treat this as report/check concurrency hygiene, not a product regression.
- Kilo job `klw_mr01nyub_7m7gz4` for desktop task suite profiles ended as `caller_timeout_after_valid_changes` with a scoped draft but no checks run. It was directionally useful, but omitted the summary surface and had weaker timeout/profile coverage than the mainline patch; do not merge or cite it as final evidence.
- OpenCode job `ocw_mr020ej4_lomowr` produced a scoped same-direction patch for requiring all seven worker MCP servers in installed-overall fresh MCP/Skill report reuse. The mainline patch kept the stricter field names `expectedWorkerMcpServers` / `missingWorkerMcpRealList`; rerun checks locally before citing the gate.
- PowerShell parser checks using `[ref]$tokens` / `[ref]$errs` require those variables to be initialized first (`$tokens=$null; $errs=$null`). An uninitialized `[ref]$errs` fails before parsing and is command-construction noise, not a script parse failure.
- OpenCode job `ocw_mr02bt5x_saqzy0` produced a same-direction desktop-task-suite approval hint patch and passed its focused check, but its schema only exposed `approvalRequired` / `staleTaskModes` / per-task rerun commands. The mainline patch kept a stricter `realTaskEvidenceRefreshPlan` with hard-vs-stale task lists, explicit real-traffic approval reason, a suite command preview, summarizer fields, and Manager support text.
- Kilo job `klw_mr02koil_o6k82z` produced a same-direction `agentic` desktop-task-suite profile patch and passed its focused check. The mainline kept the same task set (`coding-project`, `multi-turn-project`, `report-site`, `complex-project`, `repo-multi-file-edit`) plus status-doc updates and local verification.
- 2026-06-30 real agentic desktop task suite run: coding-project and multi-turn-project passed with real traffic; report-site failed by creating generic index.html/README instead of report.md with sources; complex-project artifacts were mostly correct but the verifier misread sources.json due to a UTF-8 BOM, so mainline must harden the report-site prompt and make JSON parsing BOM-safe.
- Kilo job for the real agentic desktop finding produced the intended one-line `findings.md` diff, but its check failed because the quoted `rtk rg -n "..." findings.md` command was split by the worker shell. Use single-token-safe patterns or run direct verification from the main agent before classifying this kind of worker result.
- After hardening report-site prompting, BOM-safe verifier parsing, and repair prompts, the selected real agentic refresh for `report-site,complex-project` passed with real traffic. A follow-up agentic dry-run then reported all five agentic task modes complete, fresh, and with no refresh plan.
- Goal-readiness must consume `latest-real-task-coverage-roadmap.json`, not only the five-task agentic capability matrix. Otherwise broad task coverage can be overclaimed while `extended`/`all` still need explicit-approved refreshes such as `web-search` and `free-chat`.
- Real-task roadmap refresh guidance should deduplicate task modes across profiles. When `extended` needs `web-search` and `all` needs `free-chat,web-search`, the next approval step should run only `--tasks web-search,free-chat --allow-real-traffic`, not full `extended` plus full `all`.
- Keep the real approval sequence machine-readable. The no-traffic approval plan should stitch together account setup, second-account proof, real task refresh, and release gates so the next approved traffic run does not depend on handoff prose or manual report inspection.
- Real-approval ordered actions should contain only the required release real-traffic gates. Keep optional provider/return/soak/CLI-smoke actions in a separate `optionalActions` list and expose counts in summaries, otherwise an operator approval for required release gates can accidentally look like approval for optional spend.
- Goal-readiness should consume the real-approval plan directly, not infer the next approved run only from roadmap and release-gate fragments. Require `latest-real-approval-plan.json` to be present/no-traffic with required release actions, optional action count, and next no-traffic action before treating the no-traffic goal aggregate as usable.
- Kilo worker schema paths must stay inside the worker cwd. Passing external runtime paths in `generated_paths` (for example `%APPDATA%\\anticodex\\reports`) is rejected before the job starts; keep external runtime bans in task text instead.
- Kilo job `klw_mr0h5qbg_o3xbb6` for goal-readiness real-approval consumption ended `caller_timeout_no_valid_changes` with zero diff and zero checks after the main path already passed. Do not cite it as implementation evidence; the isolated worktree was clean and removed.
- Diagnostics bundle collection is guarded to the installed Anti-Codex root. Running `collect-anticodex-diagnostics.ps1` from the source tree fails intentionally with "may only run from C:\Users\XD\AppData\Local\Anti-Codex"; use the installed runtime script with working directory `C:\Users\XD\AppData\Local\Anti-Codex` for bundle evidence.
- Installed-overall diagnostics waits must match real diagnostic bundle cost. After adding `latest-real-approval-plan.json`, diagnostic collection took about 46 seconds, so the old 20-second `DiagnosticsTimeoutSec` produced a false installed-overall failure even though standalone diagnostics passed; keep the default at 60 seconds or pass an explicit larger value.
- Kilo job `ses_0e78d6d0effeWX2tZiz5eyLbXi` for the release-gate roadmap profile-proof self-test timed out with `caller_timeout_no_valid_changes` and zero diff. Do not cite it as implementation evidence; keep this narrow self-test patch on the main path or give Kilo a longer async window.
- `test-anticodex-desktop-task-suite.mjs` used to treat `--help` as an ordinary dry-run and overwrite `latest-desktop-task-suite.json` with the default `core` profile. Keep help paths as early exits and rerun `--suite-profile agentic` after any accidental default dry-run before checking release gates.
- OpenCode job for desktop-task-suite `--no-write-report` support timed out with `caller_timeout_no_valid_changes`, zero events, and zero diff. Do not cite it as implementation evidence; use a direct main-agent patch for this small report-writing branch.
- Kilo job `ses_0e77ef734ffe6IYotR4Qbz8Ohp` for the desktop-task-suite `--no-write-report` static test timed out with `caller_timeout_no_valid_changes` and zero diff. Do not cite it as implementation evidence; patch and verify this focused regression from the main shell.
- Kilo job `klw_mr0n0uuf_i2x1ao` for the desktop task scenario catalog produced zero diff after about two minutes and was cancelled. Do not cite it as implementation evidence; keep small report-schema patches on the main path or give Kilo a longer non-blocking window.
- Full diagnostics can exceed the old 8-second per-request cap even when `/health` and `/anticodex/diagnostics?fast=1` are healthy. Keep the per-request cap aligned with the caller timeout budget instead of treating that timeout as a translator regression.
- OpenCode job `ocw_mr0ne17u_jpvas5` for the real-approval pre-login checklist produced zero diff after about two minutes and was cancelled. Do not cite it as implementation evidence; use mainline patch plus direct checks for this small report-schema task.
- 2026-06-30 ~19:55 repo-multi-file-edit real desktop task retest passed, taking about 123 seconds. This proves two rounds of multi-file editing, testing, CLI, and offline HTML generation are feasible. Keep releaseReady=false because the account/proxy release gate has not passed yet.
- 2026-06-30 ~20:02 real tool-loop refresh passed for standard tool, MCP namespace tool with real node_repl smoke, and custom/freeform apply_patch. This proves Codex-owned tool execution is still working through the Antigravity model-text path; keep releaseReady=false until accountProxyReadiness and postVerification pass.
- 2026-07-01 real agentic desktop task suite passed all five selected task modes in about 332 seconds: coding-project, multi-turn-project, report-site, complex-project, and repo-multi-file-edit. This is current direct evidence that Anti-Codex can land Codex-like long tasks through the desktop with Antigravity model text; the remaining active-goal gap is separate Antigravity account proof for `.\AntiCodexModel`.
- 2026-07-01 Claude fallback validation: Gemini low direct traffic can fail with quota `RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED`, but `claude-sonnet-4-6` passed the real small-project gate and `claude-opus-4-6-thinking` passed visible desktop report-site/complex-project evidence. Keep real verifiers model-selectable so testing can start on Gemini and switch to Claude without changing product code.
- 2026-07-01 report-site root cause: hardening only `repairLocalArtifactToolArguments` was insufficient because `deterministicArtifactToolCallCompletion` also emitted the artifact guard. If report-site creates `README.md` instead of `report.md`, check every artifact-guard entrypoint carries the task-specific `reportSite` flag before blaming model capability.
- Kilo job `klw_mr165k5p_rcqz82` produced a scoped one-file audit but timed out after valid changes and captured stale release status (`releaseReady:false`). Review worker audit docs against freshly rerun release/goal reports before accepting; patch stale status instead of citing worker text directly.
- `clear-anticodex-conversations.ps1 -WhatIfOnly` previously still stopped Anti-Codex processes before reporting planned moves. Dry-run cleanup must enumerate `wouldStopProcesses` and keep `stoppedProcessCount:0`; verify by running `-WhatIfOnly` while Anti-Codex is open, then `test-anticodex-ui-state.ps1` to prove the window survived.
- OpenCode job `ocw_mr16ndnv_axqbx1` was started for a docs-only second-account review in the shared worktree, then the main agent edited related scripts before the worker completed. The worker status therefore saw forbidden script/test changes and was cancelled before producing a docs file. Treat this as shared-worktree coordination failure by the main agent; do not cite the worker as review evidence.
- MiMoCode review job `mcw_mr1aefc4_9lkgt1` finished with `exit_code:0` and useful second-account proof-chain findings, but the MCP wrapper marked it failed as `no_code_changed` under the review success contract. Treat the stdout findings as advisory review evidence only, not as implementation evidence.
- `test-anticodex-manager-real-buttons.ps1 -Actions pluginCapabilities,mcpSkills` is parsed as one invalid ValidateSet value in this shell path. Use the script default action list for the plugin/MCP real-button pair, or pass actions in a shape verified by the script before relying on the command.
- Do not run `test-anticodex-goal-readiness.mjs` in parallel with a release-gates refresh when report freshness matters. Goal-readiness can read the previous release-gate report while release-gates is rewriting it; rerun goal-readiness and release-gates serially after Manager/report refreshes.
- Do not chain critical validations with plain PowerShell semicolons when the first command may fail and the later command may pass. A `goal-readiness; release-gates` chain can end with exit code 0 while the goal report printed `ok:false`; run critical gates separately or stop on `$LASTEXITCODE`.
- PowerShell `$PID` is a reserved variable. When stopping stale verifier child processes, use a different loop variable such as `$targetPid`; assigning `$pid` creates command-construction noise.
- `refresh-anticodex-local-features.ps1` does not accept `-TimeoutSec`. Bound it with the outer command timeout or add a script parameter intentionally before using that flag in recovery steps.
- OpenCode job `ocw_mr1ax84h_54czqq` was started as a docs-only audit in the shared worktree, then the main agent added the second-account readiness summary script before the worker finished. The worker correctly failed with forbidden/generated changes outside its allowed docs scope. Do not dispatch shared-worktree workers while the main agent may edit; either wait, commit first, or use an isolated worktree.
- `scripts/anticodex-manager-bridge.mjs --help` is not a fast help/status path; it enters the normal bridge flow and can wait until timeout. Use `--self-test` or bounded source inspection for quick checks instead.
- MIMO review job `dsw_mr1cqvhe_ifniya` was dispatched as no-change review, then the main agent edited the same second-account readiness files before it completed. It failed `git diff --quiet` and saw the main-agent diff as generated changes. Do not cite it as independent review evidence; use isolated worktrees or wait before editing.
- 2026-07-01 Chinese desktop task proof lesson: passing a long Chinese prompt as a PowerShell CLI argument can mojibake before Anti-Codex sees it. Keep the UI smoke verifier able to read `--prompt-file` as UTF-8, and use prompt files for Chinese real-task probes.
- 2026-07-01 project-label lesson: after the visible project label changed from `deepcodex` to `Anti-Codex`, real desktop task verification still expected a `deepcodex` project chat and produced a false failure even though artifacts/tests passed. Derive or pass the expected project label instead of hardcoding old workspace names.
- 2026-07-01 cleanup command lesson: do not combine Anti-Codex conversation cleanup and restart/follow-up commands in one PowerShell invocation. The cleanup script may stop Anti-Codex-related PowerShell processes by command line; run cleanup, then restart/verify in a separate command.
- 2026-07-01 parser-check command lesson: running `powershell.exe -Command "$tokens=..."` from an outer PowerShell shell can strip `$tokens`, `$errs`, and quoted paths before the inner parser sees them. For parser checks with variables, either use a real script file/encoded command or launch PowerShell through Node `spawnSync` with an argument array.
- 2026-07-01 second-account proof lesson: separate-account proof preconditions must run before any Antigravity real traffic. Missing IDs, matching IDs, missing approval, or not running as the target Windows user should fail with `usedRealTraffic:false`, otherwise a bad proof command can burn model quota before rejecting.
- 2026-07-01 post-login proof chain gap: the status/entrypoint/readiness scripts exposed placeholder proof commands, but no orchestrator bridged "user completed target Windows-user login" to resolved parameters and real-smoke execution. Keep `test-anticodex-second-account-post-login-proof.ps1` as the single post-login proof entrypoint; default dry-run must not leak raw account IDs.
- 2026-07-01 Chinese real-task proof: do not count English coding-project or multi-turn-project prompts as enough coverage for UI乱码/中文 instruction handling. The UI smoke defaults now use Chinese prompts and reports must carry `promptLanguage:"zh"`; multi-turn reports must also carry `followupPromptLanguage:"zh"`. Fresh real coding and multi-turn desktop runs passed with files/tests/CLI/follow-up artifacts green.
- 2026-07-01 verifier rename regression: after renaming the multi-turn follow-up prompt variable, `turnPrompts` still referenced the old `followupPrompt` name. The model had completed the task, but the verifier failed while assembling the report. Keep static tests around prompt-language/report fields when changing smoke report schemas.
- 2026-07-01 model-routing verifier lesson: after the account quota switch, all 8 model-routing real requests returned markers and `status:"success"`, but the verifier failed because CLI transport route records had empty `internalModel`. Match the tool-loop rule: require `internalModel` only for non-CLI transports; for CLI model-text routing, `requestedModel`/`resolvedModel`, marker match, `cascadeId`, and `status:"success"` are the relevant evidence.
- Command hygiene: `head` is not available in this PowerShell environment. Use `Select-Object -First` only around native PowerShell output, or run a bounded `rg`/specific file read instead.
- 2026-07-01 agentic-step-flow root cause: Gemini CLI model text was callable, but Antigravity CLI streaming degraded local-artifact tasks into prose/fake-tool claims. The fix is to detect shell-tool local artifact requests, get one non-streaming model completion for the tool turn, and relay the resulting tool call as Codex-compatible SSE. Fresh visible desktop `agentic-step-flow` with Chinese prompt and `gemini-3.5-flash-low` passed in 117.3s with real traffic, visible `阶段1`/`阶段2`, marker-scoped tool calls/outputs, exact `done.txt`, Chinese title in `index.html`, and `artifactGuardUsed:false`.
- 2026-07-01 worker coordination lesson: Kilo job `klw_mr1oiyac_6me0y1` produced a useful audit note but also changed files outside its allowed docs scope in the shared worktree. Treat that as a scope violation; review any code diff from the main shell and do not cite the worker as implementation evidence for the agentic-step-flow fix.
- 2026-07-01 complex-project root cause: after disabling deterministic artifact guards for complex projects, the internal `web_search` path still short-circuited to a deterministic web summary and never let Gemini reach external Codex tools. Complex local projects must bypass deterministic web summaries, keep external Codex tools after web prefetch, and avoid replacing real tool calls with "missing source URL" summary fallback.
- 2026-07-01 complex-project recovery proof: fresh visible desktop `gemini-3.5-flash-low` smoke `UI_COMPLEX_PROJECT_SMOKE_1782897491300` passed with real traffic in about 250.6s: 35 task tool calls, marker-scoped tool output, all seven requested files, three source URLs, Chinese report/dashboard, passing `node:test`, passing CLI JSON, exact `done.txt`, and `artifactGuardUsed:false`.
- 2026-07-01 latest Codex-parity regression: a fresh visible `complex-project` rerun with marker `UI_COMPLEX_PROJECT_SMOKE_1782902694545` created all files and passed `node:test` plus CLI JSON, but Chinese `report.md`/`index.html` content was written as question marks after the model piped literal Chinese through PowerShell into Node. A later rerun with marker `UI_COMPLEX_PROJECT_SMOKE_1782903020635` entered response history but created no artifact directory before the outer timeout, leaving an active Antigravity request/session lock that had to be cleared with the Manager `resetAntigravitySessionLock` action. Treat this as a real "does not feel like native Codex" issue: Chinese file writes must use ASCII-safe Unicode escapes/base64, and timed-out UI smokes must reset backend active requests before the next run.
- 2026-07-01 UI smoke hygiene: if Anti-Codex opens on a Codex error page after many validation chats, parse the main config first, then clear Anti-Codex conversations with backup while preserving the project root. Use `-SkipProcessStop` after manually stopping only Anti-Codex processes when an unrelated isolated account browser should stay alive.
- Command hygiene: from an outer PowerShell shell, `$_.Property` inside `powershell.exe -Command` can be stripped to `.Property` and spam thousands of errors. Prefer `wmic`, `cmd netstat/taskkill`, Node, or a real script file for process inspection and cleanup.
- 2026-07-01 complex-project UI parity lesson: marker `UI_COMPLEX_PROJECT_SMOKE_1782910299508` created all seven files, passed `node:test` and CLI JSON, and showed visible progress, but did not surface a final assistant marker because the safety repair wrote `done.txt` without printing the marker into tool output. Require complex-project UI smokes to prove marker-scoped tool output, visible progress, and final assistant marker; the repair command must print the marker after verification.
- 2026-07-01 complex-project strict recovery proof: marker `UI_COMPLEX_PROJECT_SMOKE_1782910924461` passed real visible desktop smoke in about 117s with Gemini low, `failures:[]`, two visible progress messages, two task tool calls, marker-scoped tool output, all seven files, Chinese report/dashboard, passing `node:test`, passing CLI JSON, exact `done.txt`, and final assistant text `Files created, tested, and verified.` plus the marker. This is the current proof for normal Codex-style long task UI behavior on the complex research+code+site path.
- 2026-07-01 long-task UI gate hardening: the strict complex-project smoke already required visible progress, marker-scoped tool output, task tool calls, task tool output, and final assistant marker, but upper summary gates still mostly consumed artifact/test/CLI proof. `real-task-coverage-roadmap`, `agentic-capability-matrix`, and `long-task-landing-matrix` now require those response-history proof fields for complex-project before claiming Codex-like long-task landing.
- 2026-07-02 worker MCP transport issue: in the current Codex session, MIMO Claude Coworker, MiMoCode Worker, Kilo Worker, and DeepSeek worker start attempts all returned `Transport closed` before a job id, stdout, diff, or checks. Treat this as a current MCP transport/session failure, not Anti-Codex product evidence and not a model capability result. Do not retry the same dispatch shape until the MCP session is reloaded or the worker servers are independently diagnosed.
- 2026-07-02 complex-project Chinese/UI parity recovery: asking Gemini to hand-write JavaScript Unicode escapes caused wrong Chinese such as `睟实来源`; the stable path is to let the model write real Chinese and have Anti-Codex escape raw CJK for Windows shell transport. The strict UI smoke now rejects first-person progress, literal `\n` in report/html, missing exact `真实来源`, missing marker-scoped tool output, and missing final assistant marker. Fresh marker `UI_COMPLEX_PROJECT_SMOKE_1782968784193` passed with real Gemini traffic, no repair turn, real Chinese preserved, tests/CLI green, and release gates returned `releaseReady:true`. A prior strict rerun exposed a false-green source check because `openab.com` was accepted as a generic HTTPS URL; the verifier now requires trusted `openai.com` / `*.openai.com` URLs across `sources.json`, `report.md`, and `index.html`.
- 2026-07-03 staged tool-loop gate hardening: the visible `agentic-step-flow` proof must be a formal required capability, not a side report. `agentic-capability-matrix` now requires `visible_staged_tool_loop`, release gates require the same six capability IDs, and goal-readiness requires 6/6 green capabilities before counting agentic task parity. Keep release/goal checks serial; stale report ordering can otherwise make a fresh green release look temporarily missing to goal-readiness.

The matrix/release/goal path now requires 7/7 green capabilities and checks exact 7-file output, tests, CLI JSON, trusted source URLs, Chinese report/html without mojibake, real tool calls/output, distinct visible progress, model-led execution, no artifact guard, and Codex-like process proof.
